# Layout review of the layout review proof design

The review of `hardware/proof/review-proof` by the [Layout review](../../../docs/board-design-method.md#layout-review) step of the board design method. Each round is recorded at its end, before any change to the board it calls for.

## Round 1

- **Board judged**: commit 37490fc.
- **Views**: `task hw:views -- --board review-proof.kicad_pcb --project review-proof.kicad_pro --class Mains --class Power --class Supply --class Ground --class Sensing --out <work folder>/review-proof-views`, run from `hardware/proof/review-proof/`. No other view was rendered in this round.
- **Pre-order check**: the board's `DESIGN` line, and every `VIOLATION` line under it. No `FIGURE` line is printed under it. The check fails the board, so the review cannot close until a round in which the check passes it (step 8).

```
DESIGN | hardware/proof/review-proof/review-proof.kicad_pro | FAIL | 7 violations
VIOLATION | hardware/proof/review-proof/review-proof.kicad_pro | workmanship_figure | hardware/proof/review-proof/review-proof.kicad_pro text_variables.WORK_DETOUR_RATIO | WORK-2 (route length) has no figure: the project text variable WORK_DETOUR_RATIO is not set, so the rule cannot be measured; set it to the figure the board's rules table gives for WORK-2, a plain decimal number of times the shortest straight-line tree joining the net's pads
VIOLATION | hardware/proof/review-proof/review-proof.kicad_pro | workmanship_figure | hardware/proof/review-proof/review-proof.kicad_pro text_variables.WORK_STUB_MM | WORK-6 (stub) has no figure: the project text variable WORK_STUB_MM is not set, so the rule cannot be measured; set it to the figure the board's rules table gives for WORK-6, a plain decimal number of mm of track ending free
VIOLATION | hardware/proof/review-proof/review-proof.kicad_pro | workmanship_figure | hardware/proof/review-proof/review-proof.kicad_pro text_variables.WORK_NECK_MM | WORK-8 (narrowing) has no figure: the project text variable WORK_NECK_MM is not set, so the rule cannot be measured; set it to the figure the board's rules table gives for WORK-8, a plain decimal number of mm from a pad within which a track may be narrower than its net class's track width
VIOLATION | hardware/proof/review-proof/review-proof.kicad_pro | workmanship_figure | hardware/proof/review-proof/review-proof.kicad_pro text_variables.WORK_ANGLE_TOLERANCE_DEG | WORK-9 (direction) has no figure: the project text variable WORK_ANGLE_TOLERANCE_DEG is not set, so the rule cannot be measured; set it to the figure the board's rules table gives for WORK-9, a plain decimal number of degrees off the board's edges or 45 degrees to them, and off a long pad's axis
VIOLATION | hardware/proof/review-proof/review-proof.kicad_pro | workmanship_figure | hardware/proof/review-proof/review-proof.kicad_pro text_variables.WORK_PAD_ENTRY_RATIO | WORK-10 (pad entry) has no figure: the project text variable WORK_PAD_ENTRY_RATIO is not set, so the rule cannot be measured; set it to the figure the board's rules table gives for WORK-10, a plain decimal number of times as long as wide, from which a pad is entered only along its long axis
VIOLATION | hardware/proof/review-proof/review-proof.kicad_pro | workmanship_rule | hardware/proof/review-proof/review-proof.kicad_dru | WORK-4 (corner) has no rule: the design's rules file holds no (constraint track_angle ...), so KiCad does not measure it; add a rule holding (constraint track_angle (min <angle>), a bare number of degrees) at the figure the board's rules table gives for WORK-4
VIOLATION | hardware/proof/review-proof/review-proof.kicad_pro | workmanship_rule | hardware/proof/review-proof/review-proof.kicad_dru | WORK-5 (jog) has no rule: the design's rules file holds no (constraint track_segment_length ...), so KiCad does not measure it; add a rule holding (constraint track_segment_length (min <length>mm)) at the figure the board's rules table gives for WORK-5

### Findings

| Finding | Raised by | Severity | What and where | Rule | Added as | Planning shortfall | Closed by |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1.1 | FLOOR-3 | quality | U4, the controller, sits 28 mm from U2, the regulator that feeds it: centres (44, 14) and (72, 14), courtyards 21.0 mm apart (x 48.4 to 69.4). Only C2, and TP2 on B.Cu, lie between them. `+3V3` therefore crosses 21.4 mm of open board on F.Cu at y 14 to 15.8, from U2's tab (x 47.15) to C4 (x 68.5). Views: assembly-top-r1c1, -r1c2; net-_+3V3-r1c1, -r1c2. | no: how close the parts can sit depends on everything else the board must place, so it needs judgement on the views | FLOOR-3 | — | change: commit 8e74e74, which moves the controller and its crystal 9.5 mm toward the regulator, 11.4 mm from it with nothing between; round 8 judges it |
| 1.2 | POWER-3 | function | The load's 1.0 A return (constraints.md, load supply continuous current, which returns through ground) passes from R1's pad 2 at (63.96, 52) into the In1.Cu and In2.Cu planes through one via of its own, at (65.5, 52.6). That via is 0.9/0.35 mm and is reached by a 1.54 mm track, 0.6 mm wide. The next ground vias are TP5's at (66.5, 55.5), 4.3 mm away, and TP6's at (69.5, 55.5), 6.6 mm away. One via per 200 mA asks for 5. The plan's POWER-3 row says no power path changes layer, but names only `+5V`, `LOAD_RTN`, `+3V3` and the mains nets. Views: net-_GND-r2c2, net-_LOAD_RTN-r2c2; facts.txt (`/GND` 17 vias). | no: which vias share a path's current depends on where the current flows, which the board's files do not state | POWER-3 | POWER-3 (catalogue item): its Judged on and Passes speak only of power-class nets, so a load return carried by the ground class went unplanned. Amend it to cover the return path of every supply or load current, whatever its class. Amended in commit 8e74e74. | change: commit 8e74e74, which gives the load's return five vias at R1's ground pad; round 8 judges it |
| 1.3 | DECOUPLE-1 | function | `+5V` reaches U2's input, pin 3 at (40.85, 16.3), before it reaches C1, the 1 µF input capacitor. The trunk branches at (37, 16.3) straight to pin 3's pad, and only then runs 2.95 mm on to C1's pad 1 at (40.85, 19.25). C1 therefore hangs on a spur beyond the pin instead of the supply entering through its pad. Views: crop-U2, net-_+5V-r1c1. | no: which pin a capacitor serves, and where its supply comes from, needs judgement on the views | DECOUPLE-1 | Planning the layout, steps 2 and 3. A `yes` row need only say how the layout meets the item, and step 3 checks only that the parts it names exist. So the row's "C1 (1 µF) at U2's input pin 3" passed without stating where the supply enters, although the same row states it for C4. Amend step 2 so that a `yes` row meets each clause of the item's Passes for every part it binds. Amended in commit 8e74e74. | change: commit 55939ad, which redraws the board; the next round judges it |
| 1.4 | CLOCK-1 | function | Y1's load capacitors sit at the two ends of the crystal, not side by side: C6 at (84.4, 9.75) and C5 at (84.4, 18.25) are 8.5 mm apart centre to centre, with courtyards 7.0 mm apart. Each sits beyond its crystal pad as seen from U4, so `XTAL1` and `XTAL2` cross Y1's pads to reach them. C5, the input's capacitor, is 11.1 mm from U4 pin 2, farther than Y1's pad 1 at 7.1 mm. Each capacitor grounds through its own via, (86.5, 18.25) and (86.5, 9.75), 8.5 mm apart. Views: crop-U4, assembly-top-r1c2, layer-F.Cu-r1c2. | no: which parts are a crystal's load capacitors, and whether they sit together, needs judgement on the views | CLOCK-1 | Planning the layout, step 2, as for finding 1.3. The CLOCK-1 row placed C5 "at Y1's pad 1" and C6 "at Y1's pad 2" and never addressed the item's "the two capacitors sit side by side". The amendment finding 1.3 names covers it. Amended in commit 8e74e74. | change: commit 46ee1d3; the next round judges it |
| 1.5 | CLOCK-3 | function | Unrelated parts sit within 25 mm of the crystal. Measured from Y1's courtyard, (77.15, 7.25)–(82.85, 20.75), to each part's courtyard: FB1 17.8 mm, C3 20.3 mm, U3 22.7 mm and R2 23.2 mm on F.Cu, and TP2 23.2 mm on B.Cu. The analog sensing region's bound at y 28 is 7.3 mm from Y1's courtyard. The plan row's "no part … within 25 mm" holds only measured from Y1's centre, (80, 14). Views: assembly-top-r1c2, -r2c2; assembly-bottom-r1c2. | yes: a custom rule in the rules file holding `(constraint courtyard_clearance (min 25mm))`, conditioned on A being the crystal and B being a footprint that is not one of the oscillator's own parts. It is to be confirmed loaded by step 3 of Rules before routing. | CLOCK-4 (catalogue item, rule-kind), which carries the custom rule, and CLOCK-3 (catalogue item), which keeps the judgement | CLOCK-3 (catalogue item): it does not say where the 25 mm is measured from, and the plan measured it from the crystal's centre. Amend it to measure from the crystal's body to each other part's body, as its source's "closer than 1 inch to the crystal" reads. Amended in commit 8e74e74. | change: commit 8e74e74, which moves the bead FB1 and the capacitor C3 beyond 25 mm of the crystal; round 8 judges it |
| 1.6 | EMI-1 | quality | The load's supply and its return take different ways across the board. `+5V` runs from J2 down x 37 to J4 (y 3.5 to 55.5). The return leaves J4's pin 2 at (39.5, 55.5), runs east along y 52 to R1 at x 58 to 64, and reaches J2's pin 2 at (39.5, 3.5) only through the ground copper from there. The loop of J2, the trunk, J4, R1 and the straight ground path back to J2 encloses about 720 mm². ## Round 2

- **Board judged**: commit 55939ad.
- **Views**: `task hw:views -- --board review-proof.kicad_pcb --project review-proof.kicad_pro --class Mains --class Power --class Supply --class Ground --class Sensing --out <work folder>/review-proof-views`, run from `hardware/proof/review-proof/`. No other view was rendered in this round.
- **Pre-order check**: the board's `DESIGN` line and every `FIGURE` line under it. No `VIOLATION` line is printed under it, and the check passes the board.

```
DESIGN | hardware/proof/review-proof/review-proof.kicad_pro | PASS
FIGURE | hardware/proof/review-proof/review-proof.kicad_pro | WORK-2 route length | WORK_DETOUR_RATIO | 1.3 times the shortest straight-line tree joining the net's pads
FIGURE | hardware/proof/review-proof/review-proof.kicad_pro | WORK-6 stub | WORK_STUB_MM | 0 mm of track ending free
FIGURE | hardware/proof/review-proof/review-proof.kicad_pro | WORK-8 narrowing | WORK_NECK_MM | 1 mm off its net's pads that a track narrower than its net class's track width may run
FIGURE | hardware/proof/review-proof/review-proof.kicad_pro | WORK-9 direction | WORK_ANGLE_TOLERANCE_DEG | 0.1 degrees off the board's edges or 45 degrees to them, and off a long pad's axis
FIGURE | hardware/proof/review-proof/review-proof.kicad_pro | WORK-10 pad entry | WORK_PAD_ENTRY_RATIO | 2 times as long as wide, from which a pad is entered only along its long axis
```

### Verdicts

| Item | Kind | Severity | Verdict | Rests on | Why |

### Findings

| Finding | Raised by | Severity | What and where | Rule | Added as | Planning shortfall | Closed by |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 2.1 | FLOOR-1 | function | J4, the load connector, crosses out of its region. The floorplan puts J4 in the low-voltage region, (28, 0)–(56, 60), but its courtyard spans x 49.35 to 57.75, y 52.65 to 59.40. It reaches 1.75 mm past the x 56 bound into the analog sensing region, to within 0.32 mm of R1's courtyard (x 58.07). Its pins at (52.3, 55.5) and (54.8, 55.5), and their pads (to x 55.65), lie in the low-voltage region; only its body crosses. Views: assembly-top-r2c2, classes-r2c2. | yes: a custom rule in the rules file, one per floorplan region. A rule area is drawn at the region's bounds, and `(constraint disallow footprint)` applies to any footprint outside that region's block whose courtyard intersects it (`A.intersectsArea('<region>')`). The parts that bridge the barrier are excepted. | FLOOR-4 (catalogue item, rule-kind) | — | change: commit 7c58e6a; the next round judges it |
| 2.2 | SENSE-1 | function | R3, U3's gain resistor, does not return to ground at the shunt's ground pad. It has a 0.5 mm `GND` track of its own from its pad 2 at (71.575, 49.425), down x 71.575 and west along y 53.96 to R1's pad 2 at (60, 53.96). But that track, R3's pad 2 and R1's pad 2 all lie inside the F.Cu ground pour, which joins them solidly along their whole length: layer-F.Cu-r2c2 shows no outline round any of them. The pour is also the copper by which the load's 1.0 A leaves R1's pad 2. So R3 in effect returns to ground at its own pad, 12.4 mm from R1's ground pad, and its own track adds nothing that is apart from the load current. Views: layer-F.Cu-r2c2, net-_GND-r2c2, net-_AMP_FB-r2c2. | no: where in a ground pour the load current flows, and whether a return within the pour is apart from that current, needs judgement on the views | SENSE-1 | — | change: commit 46ee1d3; the next round judges it |
| 2.3 | CLOCK-1 | function | The crystal's input and output tracks run side by side. `XTAL1` (the input, U4 pin 2) and `XTAL2` (the output, U4 pin 5) leave U4 together and run in parallel east along y 14.5 and y 13, from x 73.925 to x 79.2. That is 5.28 mm at 1.5 mm centre to centre, 1.25 mm apart edge to edge, with a strip of the F.Cu ground pour between them. They turn apart only at Y1's centre, to reach its pads. The plan's CLOCK-1 row says they are "routed apart". Views: crop-U4, layer-F.Cu-r1c2. | no: how soon two tracks must part, when they leave pins 1.5 mm apart for crystal pads 8.5 mm apart, needs judgement, and the source gives no figure | CLOCK-1 | — | change: commit 46ee1d3; the next round judges it |
| 2.4 | MAINS-3 | safety | The mains copper areas reach under U1, between its two rows of pins, on F.Cu and B.Cu. The `MAINS_SW` and `MAINS_L` fills run to x 26.3, 1.31 mm past the inner edge of U1's mains pads (x 24.99) towards its low-voltage pads (x 31.01). Within U1's courtyard this covers y 17.4 to 19.6 (`MAINS_SW`; to 20.3 on B.Cu) and y 22.6 to 25.55 (`MAINS_L`; from 23.3 on B.Cu), under U1's body. The surface distance across U1, from mains copper to its low-voltage pads, drops from the part's own 6.02 mm to 4.71 mm. The 4 mm creepage rule still holds. The plan's MAINS-3 row is not met: "No copper on any layer between U1's mains pins (x 24.19 mm) and its low-voltage pins (x 31.81 mm)". ### Findings

| Finding | Raised by | Severity | What and where | Rule | Added as | Planning shortfall | Closed by |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 3.1 | plan: WORK-4 | quality | `AMP_OUT` branches at a via, not at a pad. Three `AMP_OUT` tracks meet at the via at (73.225, 43.45), which lies on no pad, 1.5 mm north of R2's pad 1 (73.225, 44.95). They are: the B.Cu run from U4 pin 14 arriving down x 73.225; the B.Cu run leaving south-west and west along y 44.45 under U3 to the via at (65.3, 43.05) and U3 pin 1; and the F.Cu track south into R2's pad 1. The plan's WORK-4 row says "every branch at a pad". KiCad's `track_angle` compares only the two B.Cu tracks there (135°), and `WORK_PAD_ANGLE_DEG` measures only at pads, so the check holds nothing at this branch. Views: net-_AMP_OUT-r2c2, layer-B.Cu-r2c2, crop-U3. | yes: a measured rule. Every point where three or more of a net's tracks meet, counting tracks on different layers that a via joins there, must lie on the copper of one of the net's pads, against a figure of 0 such points. | WORK-4, which already states it ("A branch is made at a pad"). Its Corners rule and `WORK_PAD_ANGLE_DEG` do not measure a branch at a via that joins layers. | — | met: a branch where a route changes layer at a via, with two tracks on one layer there turning at 45 degrees, makes no corner KiCad's rule or the item fails; the item's opening and the plan's WORK-4 row now say a route branches at a pad or at such a via |

## Round 4

- **Board judged**: commit 2960ebd, whose design files are those commit 46ee1d3 left.
- **Views**: `task hw:views -- --board review-proof.kicad_pcb --project review-proof.kicad_pro --class Mains --class Power --class Supply --class Ground --class Sensing --out <work folder>/review-proof-views`, run from `hardware/proof/review-proof/`. No other view was rendered in this round.
- **Pre-order check**: the board's `DESIGN` line and every `FIGURE` line under it. No `VIOLATION` line is printed under it, and the check passes the board.

```
DESIGN | hardware/proof/review-proof/review-proof.kicad_pro | PASS
FIGURE | hardware/proof/review-proof/review-proof.kicad_pro | WORK-2 route length | WORK_DETOUR_RATIO | 1.3 times the shortest straight-line tree joining the net's pads
FIGURE | hardware/proof/review-proof/review-proof.kicad_pro | WORK-4 branch at a pad | WORK_PAD_ANGLE_DEG | 90 degrees at least between two of three or more tracks of a net that leave a pad on one layer
FIGURE | hardware/proof/review-proof/review-proof.kicad_pro | WORK-8 narrowing | WORK_NECK_MM | 1 mm off its net's pads that a track narrower than its net class's track width may run
FIGURE | hardware/proof/review-proof/review-proof.kicad_pro | WORK-9 direction | WORK_ANGLE_TOLERANCE_DEG | 0.1 degrees off the board's edges or 45 degrees to them, and off a long pad's axis
FIGURE | hardware/proof/review-proof/review-proof.kicad_pro | WORK-10 pad entry | WORK_PAD_ENTRY_RATIO | 2 times as long as wide, beyond which a pad is entered only along its long axis
FIGURE | hardware/proof/review-proof/review-proof.kicad_pro | WORK-4 corner at a pad | the rules file's track_angle | 135 degrees at least between two tracks of a net that meet at a pad, as at any other joint
```

### Plan and free pass

- **Plan**: each region holds the parts the floorplan gives it, within its bounds (FLOOR-1), and J1, J2 and J4 sit at (6, 24), (37, 3.5) and (50.3, 55.5), on the left, top and bottom edges. Every `yes` row holds on the board except three that open findings record: the FLOOR-1 row's "U4 beside U2" (finding 1.1), the CLOCK-3 row's "no part … within 25 mm of its body", which itself names FB1 and C3 within it and leaves out TP2 at 23.25 mm (finding 1.5), and the MAINS-3 row (finding 2.4).
- **Free pass**: finding 4.2. Findings 1.49, 1.50, 1.51 and 2.8 are still open, as the board stands: TP5 and TP6 reach R1's pad 2 only through the planes, by their vias at (66.5, 55.5) and (69.5, 55.5); U4's four paste openings are centred on its four thermal vias; the board has no mounting holes; and the ground zones join pads in full, J2 pin 2 on all four layers and one pad each of C1 to C6, R4, D1 and R1 to the F.Cu pour. R3's ground pad now lies in the rule area, off the pour.

### Findings

| Finding | Raised by | Severity | What and where | Rule | Added as | Planning shortfall | Closed by |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 4.1 | WORK-1 | quality | Y1, the crystal, is turned end-on to U4, so only its pad 1 faces the capacitors it joins. Its pads lie along x at (79.75, 14.5) and (88.25, 14.5). `XTAL1` runs 5.08 mm straight from U4 pin 2 through C5 into pad 1's near end. `XTAL2` must pass pad 1 to reach pad 2: from C6's pad 1 (75.6, 13) it turns up to y 11.5, runs 3.9 mm along pad 1's side, 1.9 mm from its copper, then crosses under Y1's body at 45° into pad 2's near end at (86.0, 14.5). That is 14.44 mm from U4 pin 5, nearly three times `XTAL1`. The turn also brings pad 2's far end to x 90.5, 1.5 mm from the board's right edge, and Y1's courtyard to 1.25 mm from it. Turned with its pads along y beyond the capacitors, as in round 3, each pad would face its own capacitor and neither track would pass the other's pad. Views: crop-U4, layer-F.Cu-r1c2, assembly-top-r1c2. | no: which way a part is turned trades its tracks against the room round it, which needs judgement on the views | WORK-1, which already states it ("each turned so that its pads face the pads they join") | — | change: commit 67e70a4; the next round judges it |
| 4.2 | free pass | quality | The F.Cu ground pour stops 0.077 mm short of R3's ground return track, less than the board's 0.15 mm minimum clearance. The `R3 ground return` rule area's south-west edge, from (62.3, 49.2) to (64.2, 47.3), runs parallel to the track's 45° run from (64.06, 47.9) to (62.96, 49.0), 0.327 mm from its centre line, and the track is 0.5 mm wide. The fill follows that edge, so along the 1.56 mm run, and round the end of the run along y 47.9, a slot 0.077 mm wide parts two pieces of `GND` copper. That is narrower than the 0.15 mm spacing JLCPCB states for this board (design-rules.md, Minimum clearance), so the slot may close in etching, joining R3's return to the pour 0.3 to 1.9 mm before R1's pad 2, or leave a sliver. Elsewhere the area keeps 0.275 to 0.632 mm from the track and R3's pad. KiCad's layout rule check does not compare copper of one net, so the pre-order check passes it. Views: layer-F.Cu-r2c2 and net-_GND-r2c2 at (63.2, 48.4), where the slot is about 1.5 pixels wide, under the area's drawn outline; measured from the layout file's fill. | yes: a measured rule. On every copper layer, no gap between a zone's fill and copper of its own net that the fill stops short of is narrower than the board's minimum clearance. KiCad's `physical_clearance` compares copper of one net, but it also reports every two pieces that touch (KiCad 10.0.7, on a copy of this board), so it cannot state this. | MFG-6 (catalogue item, rule-kind) | — | change: commit 67e70a4; the next round judges it |

## Round 5

- **Board judged**: commit 3e57d79, whose design files are those commit 67e70a4 left.
- **Views**: `task hw:views -- --board review-proof.kicad_pcb --project review-proof.kicad_pro --class Mains --class Power --class Supply --class Ground --class Sensing --out <work folder>/review-proof-views`, run from `hardware/proof/review-proof/`. No other view was rendered in this round.
- **Pre-order check**: the board's `DESIGN` line and every `FIGURE` line under it. No `VIOLATION` line is printed under it, and the check passes the board.

```
DESIGN | hardware/proof/review-proof/review-proof.kicad_pro | PASS
FIGURE | hardware/proof/review-proof/review-proof.kicad_pro | WORK-2 route length | WORK_DETOUR_RATIO | 1.3 times the shortest straight-line tree joining the net's pads
FIGURE | hardware/proof/review-proof/review-proof.kicad_pro | WORK-4 branch at a pad | WORK_PAD_ANGLE_DEG | 90 degrees at least between two of three or more tracks of a net that leave a pad on one layer
FIGURE | hardware/proof/review-proof/review-proof.kicad_pro | WORK-8 narrowing | WORK_NECK_MM | 1 mm off its net's pads that a track narrower than its net class's track width may run
FIGURE | hardware/proof/review-proof/review-proof.kicad_pro | WORK-9 direction | WORK_ANGLE_TOLERANCE_DEG | 0.1 degrees off the board's edges or 45 degrees to them, and off a long pad's axis
FIGURE | hardware/proof/review-proof/review-proof.kicad_pro | WORK-10 pad entry | WORK_PAD_ENTRY_RATIO | 2 times as long as wide, beyond which a pad is entered only along its long axis
FIGURE | hardware/proof/review-proof/review-proof.kicad_pro | WORK-4 corner at a pad | the rules file's track_angle | 135 degrees at least between two tracks of a net that meet at a pad, as at any other joint
```

### Plan and free pass

- **Plan**: each region holds the parts the floorplan gives it, within its bounds (FLOOR-1), and J1, J2 and J4 sit at (6, 24), (37, 3.5) and (50.3, 55.5), on the left, top and bottom edges. Every `yes` row holds on the board except three that open findings record: the FLOOR-1 row's "U4 beside U2" (finding 1.1), the CLOCK-3 row, which itself names FB1, TP2 and C3 within 25 mm of Y1's body at 20.6, 23.1 and 24.5 mm, as measured (finding 1.5), and the MAINS-3 row (finding 2.4). The CONN-2 row still calls U4 the board's only leadless part; Y1, now a ceramic crystal with its pads under its body, is leadless too, and it also sits more than 10 mm from every connector and edge, so what the row commits the layout to holds. The CLOCK-2 row's 0.9 mm is from each capacitor's ground pad to its via's centre, 1.35 mm centre to centre. The CLOCK-1, EMI-2, WORK-1 and WORK-10 rows' new figures hold as written.
- **Free pass**: finding 4.2 is met by the change. The `R3 ground return` area's south-west edge now runs parallel to the track's 45° run, and its north and south edges parallel to its run along y 47.9, each 0.6 mm from the track's centre line, so the pour stops 0.35 mm from the track's copper along its whole length within the area. Finding 5.1. Findings 1.49, 1.50, 1.51 and 2.8 are still open, as the board stands: TP5 and TP6 reach R1's pad 2 only through the planes, by their vias at (66.5, 55.5) and (69.5, 55.5); U4's four paste openings are centred on its four thermal vias; the board has no mounting holes; and the ground zones join pads in full, J2 pin 2 on all four layers and one pad each of C1 to C6, R4, D1 and R1 to the F.Cu pour. The F.Cu pour also comes within about 0.05 mm of each corner of U4's exposed pad, diagonally between the corner pins' clearances, where the fill cannot reach at its 0.25 mm minimum thickness. The pad is joined to the pour along pin 8 and through its four vias, so a bridge there joins nothing not already joined; it is not recorded as a finding.

### Findings

### Plan and free pass

- **Plan**: each region holds the parts the floorplan gives it, within its bounds (FLOOR-1), and J1, J2 and J4 sit at (6, 24), (37, 3.5) and (50.3, 55.5), on the left, top and bottom edges. Every `yes` row holds on the board except three that open findings record: the FLOOR-1 row's "U4 beside U2" (finding 1.1), the CLOCK-3 row, which itself names FB1, TP2 and C3 within 25 mm of Y1's body at 20.6, 23.1 and 24.5 mm, as measured (finding 1.5), and the MAINS-3 row (finding 2.4). The CONN-2 row's new wording holds: U4 and Y1 are the board's leadless parts, at (72, 14) and (79.2, 13.75), their courtyards 11.4 mm and 10.65 mm from the nearest edge and 27.2 mm and 34.8 mm from the nearest connector, J2. The CLOCK-2 row's new 1.35 mm holds: C5's and C6's ground pads at (75.6, 16.05) and (75.6, 11.45) are each 1.35 mm, centre to centre, from their vias at (75.6, 17.4) and (75.6, 10.1). The SENSE-1 row holds with the wider rule area.
- **Free pass**: finding 5.1 is met by the change. The `R3 ground return` area's north and south edges now run along y 47.1 and y 48.7, 0.325 mm from R3's pad 2 and 0.8 mm from the track's centre line, its east edge stays at x 70.6, 0.275 mm from the pad, and its two 45° edges stay 0.6 mm from the track's 45° run. Measured from the layout file's F.Cu fill, the narrowest gap between the pour and R3's pad 2 is 0.275 mm, at its east end, and between the pour and R3's return track, away from where it meets R1's pad 2, 0.35 mm, along the 45° run; both are at least the board's 0.15 mm minimum clearance. The fill outside the area is as round 5 judged it. Findings 1.49, 1.50, 1.51 and 2.8 are still open, as the board stands: TP5 and TP6 reach R1's pad 2 only through the planes, by their vias at (66.5, 55.5) and (69.5, 55.5); U4's four paste openings are centred on its four thermal vias; the board has no mounting holes; and the ground zones join pads in full, J2 pin 2 on all four layers and one pad each of C1 to C6, R4, D1 and R1 to the F.Cu pour. The F.Cu pour also comes within about 0.05 mm of each corner of U4's exposed pad, diagonally between the corner pins' clearances, where the fill cannot reach at its 0.25 mm minimum thickness. The pad is joined to the pour along pin 8 and through its four vias, so a bridge there joins nothing not already joined; it is not recorded as a finding.

### Findings

This round raises no finding.

### Plan and free pass

- **Plan**: each region holds the parts the floorplan's Parts column gives it, within its bounds (FLOOR-1; the placement measures list the same four regions with the same parts and `OUTSIDE` 0), and J1, J2 and J4 sit at (6, 24), (37, 3.5) and (50.3, 55.5), on the left, top and bottom edges. Every `yes` row holds on the board except three that open findings record: the FLOOR-1 row's "U4 beside U2" (finding 1.1), the CLOCK-3 row, which itself names FB1, TP2 and C3 within 25 mm of Y1's courtyard at 20.56, 23.10 and about 24.5 mm, as measured (finding 1.5), and the MAINS-3 row (finding 2.4). The CONN-2 row holds: U4 and Y1 are the board's leadless parts, at (72, 14) and (79.2, 13.75), their courtyards 11.4 mm and 10.65 mm from the nearest edge and 27.2 mm and 34.8 mm from the nearest connector, J2. The CLOCK-2 row's 1.35 mm holds: C5's and C6's ground pads at (75.6, 16.05) and (75.6, 11.45) are each 1.35 mm, centre to centre, from their vias at (75.6, 17.4) and (75.6, 10.1). The SENSE-1 row holds with its rule area.
- **Free pass**: findings 1.49, 1.50, 1.51 and 2.8 are still open, as the views show: TP5 and TP6 reach R1's pad 2 only through the planes, by their vias at (66.5, 55.5) and (69.5, 55.5), with nothing on net-_GND-r2c2 joining them to the pad apart from the pour; U4's four paste openings on layer-F.Paste-r1c2 are centred on its four open thermal vias, which crop-U4 shows; layer-Edge.Cuts-r1c1 and the assembly views show no mounting hole on the board; and J2 pin 2 is joined in full to the ground copper on layer-F.Cu-r1c1, layer-In1.Cu-r1c1, layer-In2.Cu-r1c1 and layer-B.Cu-r1c1, with no relief spokes, as are one pad each of C1 to C6, R4, D1 and R1 to the F.Cu pour. layer-F.SilkS-r1c1, -r1c2 and -r2c2 show nothing else wrong with the silkscreen. The F.Cu pour comes within about 0.05 mm of each corner of U4's exposed pad, diagonally between the corner pins' clearances, where the fill cannot reach at its 0.25 mm minimum thickness; the pad is joined to the pour along pin 8 and through its four vias, so a bridge there joins nothing not already joined, and it is not recorded as a finding. Commit ff61e91 changed the design files only by adding hidden fields: an `LCSC` field on all 26 parts and a `Rating` field on ten of them, six `50V` and four `0.25W`, on the schematic's symbols and on the layout's footprints, where each sits on the hidden F.Fab layer. No view draws them, and no footprint, track, via or zone moved, as the views here and round 6's measures agree. The schematic file, which no view shows, holds 26 placed parts and three `PWR_FLAG` symbols with hidden references, none excluded from the board or the bill of materials, none marked do-not-populate, and the layout holds the same 26 footprints. It also holds unplaced items: the library symbols `Relay_SolidState:MOC3063M` and `Regulator_Linear:AP2112K-3.3`, which no placed part uses, and hidden no-connect pins in the symbols of U4 (pins 3, 4, 6, 7, 9, 10, 13 and 16 to 20, and a hidden passive pin 21, the exposed pad's `GND`), U1 (pin 3) and the two unused symbols, while only two no-connect flags stand on the sheet. facts.txt lists 15 nets of a single unconnected pad, including U4's `~{RESET}/PB5` and `PB1`. None of this is a layout defect, since the layout joins every pad the schematic joins, so none is recorded as a finding.

### Findings

This round raises no finding.

### Plan and free pass

Judged on the design files in this folder. Plan comparison: every part lies in its plan region (the four floorplan rows, checked against the part positions in review-proof.kicad_pcb and the placement view's REGION lines: U1 is the only part straddling a bound, x 28, and is listed in both regions). The connector table is met: J1 at the left edge (6, 18.92) with its wire entries facing the edge, J2 at the top edge (37, 3.5), J4 at the bottom edge (50.3, 55.5), their courtyards 0.9, 0.65 and 0.6 mm inside the edge. Each `yes` row of the plan table was read against the coordinates in the views and files and matches: the FLOOR-3 distances (J2 to U2 12.6 mm, U2 to U4 11.4 mm), the POWER-2 trunk route, the POWER-3 five vias at x 62.962, the POWER-4 vias at (62.5, 13.1), (62.5, 14.0), (62.5, 14.9), the POWER-5 areas (x 2 to 24.19, y 8 to 20.3 and 22.6 to 34), SENSE-1 and SENSE-3 track lengths, CLOCK-1 lengths (XTAL1 and XTAL2 5.73 mm), CLOCK-3 distances (J2 25.4, U3 25.45 mm), DECOUPLE track lengths, EMI-3 ratios (2.5 and 3.0), the MFG-2 paste (59 percent), MFG-4 strip, WORK-2 figure (AMP_OUT 1.26) and the WORK-8 necks. The plan rows that say no (GROUND-4, DECOUPLE-3, -4, -5, SENSE-2, EMI-5) are borne out by the schematic. The board's own items table is empty, so there are no further rows. No plan row or floorplan row is departed from.

Free pass over every view (assembly-top and -bottom, classes, all four copper layers, Edge.Cuts, mask, paste, silkscreen, fab, courtyard, comment and adhesive layers, every net view, the four crops, the three 3D views, the placement view): nothing is raised. Things looked at and not judged defects: the drill marks drawn in layer colour on every layer for each hole (they show as discs on the F.Cu, paste and silkscreen tiles and are the same size as the Edge.Cuts hole marks, not copper or paste on through-hole pads); the plan-stated 1.8 mm opening in In1.Cu and In2.Cu at TP6 and the J4 pin 2 to TP3 stretch of LOAD_RTN over merged pad and via openings, discussed in the GROUND-2 verdict; H1 and H2 sit 2.9 mm from the mains copper (hole edge) with no item, rule or record figure for a hole in the mains region, so no defect against any stated figure; R4 and U1 courtyards 0.11 mm apart (touching, not overlapping); J4 value text on F.Fab straddling the bottom edge, which is not fabricated; the F.Adhes dot at Y1 (69.7, 13.75) from its footprint; reference designator text on F.SilkS close to the silk ticks of C7, C1, R3 and FB1 but clear of pads (the check's silkscreen clearance passes); the pour openings round J4 pads and the TP3 via. No `cannot judge` verdict remains.

### Plan and free pass

The layout meets the plan table's rows and the floorplan: regions and the connector table as read under FLOOR-1 and FLOOR-5, the `yes` rows as read under each item above. The plan's `FLOOR-4` row, one rule area and one rule per region, is met in the board and in the rules table. The plan's `EMI-5` row, `no`, is borne out by the schematic. The board's own items table holds the single row `none`, so no further item is judged. The plan does not mention `GROUND-5`, `DECOUPLE-6`, `MFG-7` or `MFG-9`, which entered the catalogue after the plan was written; the plan's table was committed before they existed and is not changed here.

Free pass, over every view: the silkscreen tiles show no designator over a pad, F.SilkS and B.SilkS read cleanly, the B.Fab value text overlaps the B.SilkS designators on `assembly-bottom` near TP3 to TP6, which is fabrication-layer text and not printed. The 3D views show every part seated with no part over a mounting hole. Nothing else is wrong on the views beyond findings 9.1 to 9.4.

## Round 10

- **Board judged**: commit 8db98f5, whose design files are unchanged since round 8; since round 9's Board judged commit, commit 8a8e9c1 added three rows to `design-rules.md`, a record and not a design file.
- **Round kind**: full
- **Views**: `task hw:views -- --board review-proof.kicad_pcb --project review-proof.kicad_pro --class Mains --class Power --class Supply --class Ground --class Sensing --out <work folder>/review-proof-views` and `task hw:placement -- --board review-proof.kicad_pcb --project review-proof.kicad_pro --out <work folder>/review-proof-placement`, both run from `hardware/proof/review-proof/`, each into a new, empty folder of its own outside every design's folder and with no KiCad project file in its path. The views command wrote 123 images, their index and the per-net fact table; the placement command wrote 4 images, its index and its measures: `LENGTH` 383.7749 mm, `CROSSINGS` 11, `OUTSIDE` 0. Further images were cut for reading only, enlargements of tile regions of the layer views (J2's pads on `layer-In1.Cu-r1c1.png`, `layer-In2.Cu-r1c1.png`, `layer-F.Cu-r1c1.png` and `layer-B.Cu-r1c1.png`, J4's on the In1.Cu tile, the `AMP_OUT` and `LED_DRV` vias on `layer-In1.Cu-r1c2.png`), cut from the tile images with an image library; they are no new layer or view and no verdict rests on them alone. The manufacturer documents fetched in this round are the JLCPCB capabilities page and PCB Assembly FAQ (Part 2) for `MFG-1`.
- **Pre-order check**: the board's `DESIGN` line and every `FIGURE` line under it, from `task hw:verify`, which ended with all 3 designs passed. No `VIOLATION` line is printed under it, and the check passes the board.

### Plan and free pass

Closures re-tested on the board as commit 8db98f5 holds it. Findings 9.1, 9.2 and 9.3, each closed by change 8a8e9c1: the change is in place, `design-rules.md` now holds the rows `Designators beside their parts`, `Decoupling vias` and `Reference designators on silkscreen`, each an inherent-zero row naming its item and quoting the check's FIGURE line, and `MFG-9`, `DECOUPLE-6` and `MFG-7` now pass, each on the report's `DESIGN` line with no `VIOLATION` line, the FIGURE line giving the figure its row gives, the row present and the views agreeing. Finding 9.4, closed by a reason: tested against the files and the method's text, the reason still holds for `GROUND-5`. The rules file has no `Plated hole spacing` rule, the rules table has no row for it, the design's files are unchanged since the round that closed its review, round 8, which raised no finding, step 5 of the layout review still says a custom-rule item holds only the boards whose rules files carry it and that a closed design which breaks it is not held to it, and the board breaks the item's figure, so `GROUND-5` is `fail` citing finding 9.4 and no new finding is raised against it. That reason answers only `GROUND-5`, a rule-kind item. It does not answer the judgement item `GROUND-2`, and finding 9.4's statement that on the layer tiles no two plated holes' rings merge is not borne out at J2: pads 1 and 2 of J2, 2.5 mm apart with pads 1.7 mm wide, leave 0.8 mm between pad edges, less than the 0.2 mm clearance round pad 1 plus the 0.5 mm thermal-relief gap round pad 2 plus the zones' 0.25 mm minimum thickness, and on the F.Cu, In1.Cu, In2.Cu and B.Cu tiles the two rings run into one opening. That is finding 10.1, raised by `GROUND-2`.

Plan comparison: the layout meets the plan table's rows and the floorplan. The regions and the connector table are met as read under `FLOOR-1` and `FLOOR-5` (the four regions' parts as the placement measures list them, 0 outside their bounds; pin 1 of J1, J2 and J4 on the table's positions). The `yes` rows are met as read under each item above, with one exception: the plan's `GROUND-2` row says the only plane openings are single clearance rings round other nets' vias and TP6's keep-out, which the layout does not meet at J2 (finding 10.1). The plan's `FLOOR-4` row, one rule area and one rule per region, is met in the board and in the rules table. The plan's `GROUND-4`, `DECOUPLE-3`, `DECOUPLE-4`, `DECOUPLE-5`, `SENSE-2` and `EMI-5` rows, `no`, are borne out by the schematic. The board's own items table holds the single row `none`, so no further item is judged. The plan does not mention `GROUND-5`, `DECOUPLE-6`, `MFG-7` or `MFG-9`, which entered the catalogue after the plan was written; the plan's table was committed before they existed and is not changed here.

Free pass, over every view: the silkscreen tiles show no designator over a pad and every part named, F.SilkS and B.SilkS read cleanly; on `assembly-bottom` the B.Fab value text overlaps the B.SilkS designators near TP3 to TP6, which is fabrication-layer text and not printed; the drill marks drawn in layer colour for each hole on the layer tiles are the holes' own marks and not copper; the 3D views show every part seated, no part over a mounting hole. Nothing else is wrong on the views beyond finding 10.1.

### Findings

| Finding | Raised by | Severity | What and where | Rule | Added as | Planning shortfall | Closed by |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 10.1 | GROUND-2 | function | The clearance ring round J2 pad 1 (`+5V`) and the thermal-relief ring round J2 pad 2 (`GND`) run into one opening in the ground plane or pour, with no plane copper between them, on `layer-In1.Cu-r1c1.png`, `layer-In2.Cu-r1c1.png`, `layer-F.Cu-r1c1.png` and `layer-B.Cu-r1c1.png`, at board x 37.0 to 39.5, y 3.5 (the opening spans about x 35.9 to 40.6, y 2.4 to 4.6). The pads are 1.7 mm wide on a 2.5 mm pitch, 0.8 mm between pad edges, against 0.2 mm clearance plus 0.5 mm relief gap plus 0.25 mm minimum zone thickness, 0.95 mm. No track crosses the opening (the `+5V` trunk leaves pad 1 downward over solid plane from y about 4.6), but item `GROUND-2` says no two pins' rings merge into one opening, and its Passes names a pin's thermal-relief gap and clearance ring as openings like any other. J4's two pads, 2.5 mm apart, leave plane copper between their rings on all four layers. | yes, custom rule `Plated hole spacing`, form custom rule in the rules file, KiCad `hole_to_hole`, which `GROUND-5` states | GROUND-5 | — |  change: 2102a19, the four ground planes' thermal gap set to 0.25 mm and the zones refilled; every plated pair of the board swept for merging rings and only J2 pads 1 and 2 merged, which now leave 0.35 mm of plane copper between their rings |

## Round 11

- **Board judged**: commit bb7ade1, whose design files are as commit 2102a19 left them: since round 10's Board judged commit, 2102a19 set the four ground zones' thermal gap to 0.25 mm and refilled them, the only design change, and bb7ade1 and d5bf39f changed only this record.
- **Round kind**: full
- **Views**: `task hw:views -- --board review-proof.kicad_pcb --project review-proof.kicad_pro --class Mains --class Power --class Supply --class Ground --class Sensing --out <work folder>/review-proof-views` and `task hw:placement -- --board review-proof.kicad_pcb --project review-proof.kicad_pro --out <work folder>/review-proof-placement`, both run from `hardware/proof/review-proof/`, each into a new, empty folder of its own outside every design's folder and with no KiCad project file in its path. The views command wrote 123 images, their index and the per-net fact table; the placement command wrote 4 images, its index and its measures: `LENGTH` 383.7749 mm, `CROSSINGS` 11, `OUTSIDE` 0. Further images were cut for reading only, enlargements of tile regions (J2's pads on `layer-F.Cu-r1c1.png`, `layer-In1.Cu-r1c1.png`, `layer-In2.Cu-r1c1.png` and `layer-B.Cu-r1c1.png`, and the `AMP_OUT` and `LED_DRV` vias on the four `-r1c2.png` layer tiles), cut from the tile images with an image library; they are no new layer or view and no verdict rests on them alone. The manufacturer documents fetched in this round are the JLCPCB capabilities page and PCB Assembly FAQ (Part 2) for `MFG-1`.
- **Pre-order check**: the board's `DESIGN` line and every `FIGURE` line under it, from `task hw:verify`, which ended with all 3 designs passed. No `VIOLATION` line is printed under it, and the check passes the board.

```
DESIGN | hardware/proof/review-proof/review-proof.kicad_pro | PASS
FIGURE | hardware/proof/review-proof/review-proof.kicad_pro | WORK-2 route length | WORK_DETOUR_RATIO | 1.3 times the shortest straight-line tree joining the net's pads
FIGURE | hardware/proof/review-proof/review-proof.kicad_pro | WORK-4 branch at a pad | WORK_PAD_ANGLE_DEG | 90 degrees at least between two of three or more tracks of a net that leave a pad on one layer

FIGURE | hardware/proof/review-proof/review-proof.kicad_pro | MFG-7 reference on silkscreen | none, inherent zero | 0 references off a silkscreen layer or hidden
FIGURE | hardware/proof/review-proof/review-proof.kicad_pro | MFG-9 designator beside its part | none, inherent zero | 0 designators inside a part's courtyard or nearer another part than their own
FIGURE | hardware/proof/review-proof/review-proof.kicad_pro | WORK-4 corner at a pad | the rules file's track_angle | 135 degrees at least between two tracks of a net that meet at a pad, as at any other joint

### Plan and free pass

Closures re-tested on the board as commit bb7ade1 holds it. Finding 10.1, closed by change 2102a19: the change is in place (the four ground zones carry thermal_gap 0.25 mm in the board file) and `GROUND-2` now passes; J2 pads 1 and 2 leave 0.35 mm of plane copper between their rings on all four copper layers, and every other plated pair leaves copper too (J4 0.40 mm, U1 0.54 mm, the `LOAD_RTN` via beside J4 0.30 mm). Findings 9.1, 9.2 and 9.3, each closed by change 8a8e9c1: in place, `design-rules.md` holds the rows `Designators beside their parts`, `Decoupling vias` and `Reference designators on silkscreen`, and `MFG-9`, `DECOUPLE-6` and `MFG-7` pass on the report's `DESIGN` line with no `VIOLATION` line and the FIGURE line giving each row's figure. Finding 9.4, closed by a reason: tested against the files and the method text, the reason still holds for `GROUND-5`, which is therefore `fail` citing finding 9.4; no new finding is raised against it.

Plan comparison: the layout meets the plan table's rows and the floorplan, as read under each item. The regions and the connector table are met (0 parts outside their regions; pin 1 of J1, J2 and J4 on the table's positions). The plan's `GROUND-2` row, that the only openings are single clearance rings round other nets' vias and TP6's keep-out, is now met at J2 as well. The `no` rows (`GROUND-4`, `DECOUPLE-3`, `DECOUPLE-4`, `DECOUPLE-5`, `SENSE-2`, `EMI-5`) are borne out by the schematic. The board's own items table holds the single row `none`, so no further item is judged. The plan does not mention `GROUND-5`, `DECOUPLE-6`, `MFG-7` or `MFG-9`, which entered the catalogue after the plan was written; the plan's table was committed before they existed and is not changed here.

Free pass, over every view: the silkscreen tiles show no designator over a pad and every part named; on `assembly-bottom` the B.Fab value text overlaps the B.SilkS designators near TP3 to TP6, which is fabrication-layer text and not printed; the drill marks drawn on the layer tiles are the holes' own marks and not copper; the 3D views show every part seated, no part over a mounting hole. Nothing else is wrong on the views.

This round raises no finding.

### Plan and free pass

Plan comparison. Every region's bounds, parts and connector position in layout-plan.md match the layout and views; the coordinates the plan quotes for vias, tracks and parts (five return vias, U4's three thermal vias, test points, AMP_OUT and LED_DRV routes, XTAL lengths, the three decoupling positions) all match. Departures: the GROUND-4 row says `no`, but the item's condition (an integrated circuit with a ground pin) is met, and the DECOUPLE-2 row promises a single via per capacitor, which that item does not accept; the plan has no rows for FLOOR-5, GROUND-5, DECOUPLE-6, MFG-7, MFG-8 and MFG-9, and for GROUND-5 the layout carries nothing. The board's own items table is `none`.

Free pass. Looked at every layer view, the nets, crops, assembly, 3D and the placement view. Seen and not raised: TP3 (54.8, 55.5) stands under J4's outline and 0.65 mm pad to pad from its pin 2 (tall part on the far side); J2 has no mounting hole within 40 mm; the F.Cu ground pour is cut by the +5V trunk at x 37 into a 3.6 mm strip stitched through C1, C7, R4 vias; designators U3 and R3 touch their parts' silk lines. Nothing else was found that no item asks about.

## Round 13

- **Board judged**: commit c39ba14, whose design files are as commit 47a22dd left them: since round 12's Board judged commit, 47a22dd gave U2 pin 1 and U3 pin 2 a ground via touching each pad, two ground vias to each of C1 to C4, removed the two short ground tracks, and refilled the board, the only design change; fed66be and c39ba14 changed only this record.
- **Round kind**: full
- **Views**: `task hw:views -- --board review-proof.kicad_pcb --project review-proof.kicad_pro --class Mains --class Power --class Supply --class Ground --class Sensing --out <work folder>/review-proof-views` and `task hw:placement -- --board review-proof.kicad_pcb --project review-proof.kicad_pro --out <work folder>/review-proof-placement`, both run from `hardware/proof/review-proof/`, each into a new, empty folder of its own outside every design's folder and with no KiCad project file in its path. The views command wrote 123 images, their index and the per-net fact table; the placement command wrote 4 images, its index and its measures: `LENGTH` 383.7749 mm, `CROSSINGS` 11, `OUTSIDE` 0. No further view was rendered. The manufacturer documents fetched in this round are the JLCPCB capabilities page and PCB Assembly FAQ for `MFG-1`.
- **Pre-order check**: the board's `DESIGN` line and every `FIGURE` line under it, from `task hw:verify`, which ended with all 5 designs passed. No `VIOLATION` line is printed under it, and the check passes the board.
- **Tile shorthand used in the tables**: `T4` after a view name means all four tiles `r1c1`, `r1c2`, `r2c1`, `r2c2` of that view (r1c1 covers board x -1.34 to 45.86, y -1.03 to 30.33 mm; r1c2 x 45.86 to 93.06, y -1.03 to 30.33; r2c1 x -1.34 to 45.86, y 30.33 to 61.68; r2c2 x 45.86 to 93.06, y 30.33 to 61.68). `check` means `check-report.txt`; `rules` means `design-rules.md`. The board's own items table in `layout-plan.md` holds only the row `none`, so no board-own row appears below.

### Plan and free pass

The verdict table holds exactly one row per catalogue item (67 rows), in catalogue order, and the board's own items table holds only `none`.

Comparison with `layout-plan.md`. Floorplan: the four region bounds are drawn as rule areas on all four copper layers, and `placement/measures.txt` lists `OUTSIDE | total | 0` and the four REGION lines with the plan's parts. Connector table: J1 (6, 18.92) left, J2 (37, 3.5) top, J4 (50.3, 55.5) bottom, read from the board file's footprint origins and seen on assembly-top r1c1 and r2c2. Plan rows checked against the copper and each found met: the five load-return vias (POWER-3, at the five listed coordinates), U4's three thermal vias (POWER-4), C1 to C4 ground vias (DECOUPLE-2), U2 and U3 ground vias (GROUND-4), XTAL1 and XTAL2 5.7 mm long (CLOCK-1), CLOCK-2 vias, the mains copper areas stopping at x 24.19 (POWER-5, MAINS-3, MAINS-5), TEST rows, EMI-3 track lengths, and the track widths of WORK-8. The plan has no row for FLOOR-5, GROUND-5, DECOUPLE-6, MFG-7, MFG-8 and MFG-9, since its baseline predates them; those items are judged here from the views and the report. The plan's CONN-2 row says its 10 mm figure is taken from the constraints record, and `constraints.md` carries no such figure (finding 13.3). No other plan row is departed from.

Free pass, every view opened (all tiles of the layer, classes, net, assembly and placement views; crop-U1 to crop-U4; 3d-top, 3d-bottom, 3d-angled; F.SilkS, B.SilkS, F.Paste, B.Paste (r1c2), F.Mask (r1c2) and Edge.Cuts as listed in the table). Observations that did not become findings: the AMP_OUT connection to R2 runs 14 mm of track for an 8.6 mm straight line with three vias on the net, forced by R2 serving pin 4 and by the two tracks it would otherwise cross; the rings of J4 pin 2 and the TP3 via leave 0.25 mm of plane copper; AMP_OUT's via (58.75, 14.5) and LED_DRV's via (59.1, 13) are 1.5 mm apart at U4; the 3.2 mm mounting holes leave J2 and J4 more than 30 mm from the nearest hole, which CONN-4's Passes wording allows. The `net-_LED_DRV` view named in WORK-3's judged-on text is not among the critical-class views (LED_DRV is in the Default class), so WORK-3 was judged on layer-B.Cu and layer-F.Cu for that net. The report shows no `hole_to_hole` violation because the rule is absent from the rules file, so GROUND-5 rests on the rules file and rules table. For the MFG-1 verdict the JLCPCB capabilities page and the PCB Assembly FAQ (Part 2) were fetched; the page lists "Preferred Min. Via hole size: 0.2mm", which Board Setup does not follow, and the FAQ's "more than 0.3mm" from the board edge is met by 0.3 mm only at its limit, noted and not raised.

## Round 14

- **Board judged**: commit 963b17d, whose design files are as commit d338269 left them: since round 13's Board judged commit, d338269 added the `Plated hole spacing` rule to the rules file, set Board Setup's minimum hole size to 0.2 mm and changed the records `design-rules.md`, `constraints.md` and `layout-plan.md`; the board file is unchanged.
- **Round kind**: full
- **Views**: `task hw:views -- --board review-proof.kicad_pcb --project review-proof.kicad_pro --class Mains --class Power --class Supply --class Ground --class Sensing --out <work folder>/review-proof-views` and `task hw:placement -- --board review-proof.kicad_pcb --project review-proof.kicad_pro --out <work folder>/review-proof-placement`, both run from `hardware/proof/review-proof/`, each into a new, empty folder of its own outside every design's folder and with no KiCad project file in its path. The views command wrote 123 images, their index and the per-net fact table; the placement command wrote 4 images, its index and its measures. No further view was rendered. The manufacturer documents fetched in this round are the JLCPCB capabilities page and PCB Assembly FAQ for `MFG-1`.
- **Pre-order check**: the board's `DESIGN` line and every `FIGURE` line under it, from `task hw:verify`, which ended with all 5 designs passed. No `VIOLATION` line is printed under it, and the check passes the board.
- **Tile key**: for every tiled view, r1c1 is board x -1.34 to 45.86, y -1.03 to 30.33; r1c2 x 45.86 to 93.06, y -1.03 to 30.33; r2c1 x -1.34 to 45.86, y 30.33 to 61.68; r2c2 x 45.86 to 93.06, y 30.33 to 61.68. "all tiles" means all four tiles of that view, each opened. Crops: crop-U1 x 12 to 44, y 15.9 to 27.05; crop-U2 x 27.0 to 59.0, y -0.85 to 59.8; crop-U3 x 52.9 to 84.9, y 40.7 to 47.2; crop-U4 x 45.5 to 77.5, y 9.9 to 19.8. The board's own items table in layout-plan.md is "none", so the checklist is the 67 catalogue items.

### Plan and free pass

Plan comparison: region bounds, part lists, connector positions (J1 (6, 18.92), J2 (37, 3.5), J4 (50.3, 55.5)), the plan's via coordinates, track routes and widths, and the test point positions were read against the pcb file and the views and match, with these departures. (1) FLOOR-2 and WORK-3 plan rows say AMP_OUT changes layer only to pass under the +3V3 trunk and U3; the layout also sends AMP_OUT's R2 branch under U3 and back up through a second via at (73.225, 47.6): raised as findings 1.1 and 1.2 (the plan row, followed as written, states 1.26 times the tree and does not say this leg is a detour). (2) The TEST-1 plan row considers only bodies on B.Cu; TP3 and TP4 stand under F.Cu part outlines, finding 1.3. (3) Six GND vias in the pcb are in no plan row; they stitch F.Cu pour islands and are not findings.

Free pass, every view looked over: the layer views (F.Cu, In1, In2, B.Cu, Paste, Mask, SilkS, Edge.Cuts, CrtYd, Fab, Adhes, Cmts) show no stray copper, no sliver, no text off its part and an unbroken board outline. Observations that are not findings: the F.Cu view shows a tiny dark pour void near (71.4, 43.7) and one near (65.4, 13.7), each an ordinary clearance gap; designators C3 and FB1 stand 1.3 and 1.4 mm from the other's courtyard; the 3d views show no model for H1 to H3 and TP1 to TP6, as the index says. The free pass raises no finding beyond 1.1 to 1.3.

| 14.1 | WORK-1 | quality | AMP_OUT's partner R2 (72.4, 46.375) stands on the far side of U3 from the pin it serves. On placement r2c2 and assembly-top r2c2 the AMP_OUT connection from U3 pin 1 (66.86, 43.05) to R2 pad 1 (73.225, 46.375) crosses U3's body; straight 7.2 mm, routed 12.6 mm (net-_AMP_OUT r2c2, layer-B.Cu r2c2: via (65.3, 43.05), B.Cu diagonal to (69.85, 47.6), then (73.225, 47.6)). Placement cause: the connection in placement r2c2 runs long and across U3 before any copper.
